- [lemma/docs/_llms-full.txt](lemma/docs/_llms-full.txt) — Complete raw documentation bundle
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/artifacts/delete-project-artifact.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/artifacts/delete-project-artifact.md) — Delete project artifact: Deletes one uploaded project artifact.
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/artifacts/get-agent-understanding-version.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/artifacts/get-agent-understanding-version.md) — Get Agent understanding version: Returns the Agent understanding content and metadata for a specific project, agent, and version.
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/artifacts/get-uploaded-project-artifact-content.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/artifacts/get-uploaded-project-artifact-content.md) — Get uploaded project artifact content: Returns stored text content and metadata for one uploaded text artifact.
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/artifacts/list-agent-understanding-versions.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/artifacts/list-agent-understanding-versions.md) — List Agent understanding versions: Returns Agent understanding version metadata for a project and agent, newest first. The response omits artifact content.
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/artifacts/list-project-artifacts.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/artifacts/list-project-artifacts.md) — List project artifacts: Returns Agent understanding and uploaded artifacts for a project, scoped to the selected agent.
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/artifacts/regenerate-project-artifacts.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/artifacts/regenerate-project-artifacts.md) — Regenerate project artifacts: Starts Agent understanding and diagram regeneration for a tenant-owned project and optional agent. Clients should poll list_project_artifacts for a new latest_version to observe completion.
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/artifacts/upload-project-artifact.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/artifacts/upload-project-artifact.md) — Upload project artifact: Upload an agent-scoped .txt or .md file (text/plain or text/markdown).
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/github-repositories/get-github-connection.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/github-repositories/get-github-connection.md) — Get GitHub connection: Returns retained GitHub connection health and last-known repository identity. Disabled rollout state does not remove stored history.
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/insights/get-insight.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/insights/get-insight.md) — Get insight: Get a single insight for a project, including assigned member issues. Unauthorized or unknown projects return 404.
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/insights/list-insights.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/insights/list-insights.md) — List insights: List project-scoped insights. Empty projects return an empty list. Pass limit and offset to page; defaults match issues (50 / max 200).
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/issues/attach-issue-tag.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/issues/attach-issue-tag.md) — Attach issue tag: Attach a tag to an issue idempotently.
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/issues/create-issue-tag.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/issues/create-issue-tag.md) — Create issue tag: Create a project-scoped issue tag. Names are normalized; duplicate normalized names return the existing tag with 200.
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/issues/delete-issue-tag.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/issues/delete-issue-tag.md) — Delete issue tag: Delete a project-scoped tag and cascade removal from all issues.
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/issues/detach-issue-tag.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/issues/detach-issue-tag.md) — Detach issue tag: Detach a tag from an issue idempotently.
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/issues/get-issue-markdown.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/issues/get-issue-markdown.md) — Get issue markdown: Build markdown for an issue.
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/issues/get-issue-metric-series.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/issues/get-issue-metric-series.md) — Get issue metric series: Returns zero-filled daily occurrence buckets for an issue over the last 30 days.
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/issues/get-issue.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/issues/get-issue.md) — Get issue: Get a single issue. The validation field is null when no validation row exists; when present it contains status, evidence_summary, and validated_at.
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/issues/get-issues-in-bulk.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/issues/get-issues-in-bulk.md) — Get issues in bulk: Public REST + MCP batch issue read. First-party dashboard and platform-api load issues one-at-a-time via get_issue / list_issues. Fetch many issues by id. One invalid or unauthorized id does not hide the rest of the batch. Results preserve input order. Mixed results are HTTP 200.
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/issues/list-issue-occurrences.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/issues/list-issue-occurrences.md) — List issue occurrences: List occurrences for an issue. Pass release to filter; empty string matches unreleased occurrences.
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/issues/list-issue-tags.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/issues/list-issue-tags.md) — List issue tags: List project-scoped issue tags in normalized name order.
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/issues/list-issues.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/issues/list-issues.md) — List issues: List issues for a project. By default, returns issues at or above the frequency cutoff. Pass expanded=true for less-common issues and include_context=true for dashboard metric context. expand=dashboard and projection=dashboard are deprecated aliases.
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/issues/merge-issues.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/issues/merge-issues.md) — Merge issues: Merge one active same-agent issue into this issue. The loser is dismissed as a merged duplicate; its occurrences are immediately moved onto the survivor.
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/issues/mutate-issues-in-batch.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/issues/mutate-issues-in-batch.md) — Mutate issues in batch: Apply one discriminated batch mutation: status, start-review, flag, assignee, or tag. Mixed item-level failures return HTTP 200. Successful items are not rolled back.
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/issues/patch-issue.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/issues/patch-issue.md) — Patch issue: Apply exactly one scalar mutation: status (with optional dismissal fields), flagged, or user_id assignee. Empty, mixed, or incompatible bodies are rejected before any write. start-review stays on its dedicated route.
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/issues/start-issue-review.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/issues/start-issue-review.md) — Start issue review: Set status to in_progress and assign the acting user when the issue is unassigned, in one atomic write. An existing assignee is preserved; an issue already in progress is returned unchanged.
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/linear-tickets/create-linear-ticket.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/linear-tickets/create-linear-ticket.md) — Create Linear ticket
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/linear-tickets/inspect-compact-linear-ticket-states.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/linear-tickets/inspect-compact-linear-ticket-states.md) — Inspect compact Linear ticket states: Returns no ticket history or workspace directory; only active compact states for the requested project issues.
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/linear-tickets/inspect-issue-linear-ticket.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/linear-tickets/inspect-issue-linear-ticket.md) — Inspect issue Linear ticket
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/linear-tickets/inspect-linear-connection.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/linear-tickets/inspect-linear-connection.md) — Inspect Linear connection: Returns retained Linear connection health and project defaults. Disabled rollout state does not remove stored history.
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/linear-tickets/link-confirmed-linear-ticket.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/linear-tickets/link-confirmed-linear-ticket.md) — Link confirmed Linear ticket
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/linear-tickets/preview-exact-linear-ticket.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/linear-tickets/preview-exact-linear-ticket.md) — Preview exact Linear ticket
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/linear-tickets/unlink-linear-ticket.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/linear-tickets/unlink-linear-ticket.md) — Unlink Linear ticket
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/projects/create-project.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/projects/create-project.md) — Create project: Create a project for the authenticated tenant.
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/projects/get-instrumentation-diagnostics-settings.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/projects/get-instrumentation-diagnostics-settings.md) — Get instrumentation diagnostics settings: Returns the instrumentation-diagnostics settings for a project owned by the authenticated tenant.
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/projects/get-project-analytics.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/projects/get-project-analytics.md) — Get project analytics: Returns one analytics payload selected by the required view discriminator. Specialized views do not compute unrequested panels. Cost figures are Lemma estimates from observed token counts and public LiteLLM list prices — not invoice amounts. Backfilled rows carry the price as of the backfill run, no…
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/projects/get-project-briefs-settings.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/projects/get-project-briefs-settings.md) — Get project briefs settings: Returns the Slack briefs settings for a project owned by the authenticated tenant.
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/projects/get-project.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/projects/get-project.md) — Get project: Returns a single project if it belongs to the authenticated tenant.
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/projects/inspect-project-setup-status.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/projects/inspect-project-setup-status.md) — Inspect project setup status: Returns setup state, scope, permissions, and stable blocker codes without exposing credentials or provider-private data.
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/projects/list-instrumentation-diagnostics-findings.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/projects/list-instrumentation-diagnostics-findings.md) — List instrumentation diagnostics findings: Returns the complete active instrumentation-diagnostics findings for a project owned by the authenticated tenant. Empty when the rollout gate or project preference is off.
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/projects/list-projects.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/projects/list-projects.md) — List projects: Returns projects available to the authenticated tenant.
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/projects/reorder-projects.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/projects/reorder-projects.md) — Reorder projects: Persist project ordering for the authenticated tenant.
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/projects/update-instrumentation-diagnostics-settings.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/projects/update-instrumentation-diagnostics-settings.md) — Update instrumentation diagnostics settings: Enable or disable the instrumentation-diagnostics digest for the authenticated tenant.
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/projects/update-project-briefs-settings.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/projects/update-project-briefs-settings.md) — Update project briefs settings: Enable or disable Slack briefs and optionally set cadence for the authenticated tenant.
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/projects/update-project.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/projects/update-project.md) — Update project: Update a project for the authenticated tenant.
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/traces/bulk-delete-traces.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/traces/bulk-delete-traces.md) — Bulk delete traces: Delete multiple traces for a project.
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/traces/check-per-trace-ingest-status.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/traces/check-per-trace-ingest-status.md) — Check per-trace ingest status: Returns whether a specific otel_trace_id is queued (`enqueued`), stored (`ingested`), ready (`ready`), or not found (`not_found`).
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/traces/check-ready-traces.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/traces/check-ready-traces.md) — Check ready traces: Returns whether a project has ready traces.
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/traces/delete-trace.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/traces/delete-trace.md) — Delete trace: Delete a trace for a project.
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/traces/discover-trace-search-facets.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/traces/discover-trace-search-facets.md) — Discover trace search facets: Returns curated, bounded facets for the active trace-search context.
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/traces/get-trace.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/traces/get-trace.md) — Get trace: Loads a single trace with spans and stats. Pass expand=context to return the agent context bundle (row, stats, spans, and issue occurrences) instead of the default detail payload. Default (no expand) responds with the get_trace detail schema; expand=context responds with the get_trace_context bundle…
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/traces/list-thread-issue-summaries.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/traces/list-thread-issue-summaries.md) — List thread issue summaries: Batch-fetch issue extraction completion and dev issues for thread_ids (join key for eval trial_hash).
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/traces/list-thread-traces.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/traces/list-thread-traces.md) — List thread traces: Thread traversal for a ready trace. scope=all (default) returns every ready member oldest first using the threadTraces schema; scope=neighbors returns previous/next ids using the threadTraceNeighbors schema. Empty for standalone traces.
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/traces/list-trace-agent-names.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/traces/list-trace-agent-names.md) — List trace agent names: Returns agent names seen in project traces.
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/traces/list-trace-issue-occurrences.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/traces/list-trace-issue-occurrences.md) — List trace issue occurrences: Returns issue occurrence evidence linked to a ready trace.
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/traces/list-trace-spans.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/traces/list-trace-spans.md) — List trace spans: Returns spans for a ready trace in start-time order. Optional limit/offset pages the result and include_attributes/include_events=false drop the payload-heavy span keys. Default returns every span with all fields. The bare array is intentional (continuation is detected by a short or empty page, no t…
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/traces/list-traces.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/traces/list-traces.md) — List traces: Canonical first-party dashboard list with cursor pagination and independent has_error/has_issues filters. Kept separate from GET /traces because that legacy window list uses a different backend and response shape.
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/traces/search-trace-evidence.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/traces/search-trace-evidence.md) — Search trace evidence: Search visible span content, grouped into trace-level results. Distinct from structural search.
- [lemma/docs/api-reference/traces/search-traces-structural.md](lemma/docs/api-reference/traces/search-traces-structural.md) — Search traces (structural): Structural search over traces and span attributes. Distinct from evidence search, which searches visible span content.
- [lemma/docs/connections/linear.md](lemma/docs/connections/linear.md) — Linear: Create, link, and unlink Linear tickets from Lemma issues
